## Witness and red checks

- Tracked total at the start of S1: 2132. Class counts: keep 747, runtime 76, dead 94, generated 654, duplicate 561. Sum 2132.
- Bytes per class: keep 34222064, runtime 432192, dead 16525722, generated 1340448, duplicate 418262.
- Red check, unclassified paths: 0.

## Rules

- keep: under src, dsp, effects, kernel, image, config, vendor, ci or .github/workflows; an owner root document (AGENTS.md, README.md, CLAUDE.md, LICENSE, .gitignore, .gitattributes, docs/PENDING_VALIDATION.md); an operator tool listed below; or reached from a kept file by its repo path, a two-component path suffix, a unique basename, a require/import, a C/C++ include, a Faust import, or a directory under test-audio-corpus, vendor, docs/demo or tools that a kept file names.
- runtime: .gm, closes and .vscode state.
- duplicate: the same blob as another path, the kept or lexically first copy being canonical.
- generated: a docs/witness output that nothing kept reaches.
- dead: nothing kept reaches it.

## Operator-tool overrides

- tools/stage-readiness/check-stage.mjs: operate: owner-requested pre-set readiness command (2026-11-14); no doc names it yet
- tools/press-latency/press-latency.js: operate: press-to-beep harness behind archive#final-rig-timing; no doc names it yet

## Function-level findings (src), hand-checked

Method: disassembly of the CI build-binary artifact for 7a68a1ce (aloop sha256 b54a16a6, debug info): 162 `aloop::` functions, 27 with no direct branch, address load or relocation reference. Each was then grepped in src, test and tools; 22 have source call sites (inlined, leaving an orphan out-of-line copy). Unused, with no caller anywhere:

- `AudioThread::clipExporter() const` (src/dsp/audio_thread.cpp:1606, decl audio_thread.h:146)
- `AudioThread::setRealtime(int, int)` (src/dsp/audio_thread.cpp:1610, decl audio_thread.h:150)
- `AudioThread::workerLoop()` (src/dsp/audio_thread.cpp:1611, empty body, decl audio_thread.h:149; `ClipExporter::workerLoop` is a different, used function)
- `LinkBridge::recordArmMicros() const` (src/link/link_bridge.cpp:49, decl link_bridge.h:42)
- `Lv2Host::rescanUser(const std::string&)` (src/host/lv2_host.cpp:339; README.md and docs/ARCHITECTURE.md already state it has no caller)

A host `-fsyntax-only -Wall` compile of apc_grid.cpp, apc_leds.cpp, telemetry.cpp and audio_thread.cpp reports no unused static function; its unused-variable warnings in audio_thread.cpp come from the ALSA block being compiled out on this host.

## Simulator scripts CI does not run (gm.callers and grep)

- tools/loop-quantization-sim/world.js, link.js, looper.js: helpers, required by the CI cases (createWorld, createLinkWorld and stepSample have call sites in CI-run cases).
- tools/loop-quantization-sim/invariants.js: helper, required by fuzz.js and remote-join.js; runs clean on its own.
- tools/loop-quantization-sim/phrase-turnaround.js: a standalone case, required by nothing, named only by ADR-026 in docs/DECISIONS.md; it prints `phrase turnaround: FAIL (slave loops on its own take length, not the shared phrase)` and exits 1, a known model failure ADR-026 records. S3 decision: fix the model and add it to CI, or delete it with ADR-026 updated.
